Model before rewriting:

letting REC be domain variant {a: int(-2147483647..2147483647), b: int(-2147483647..2147483647)}
letting p be {variant{a = 10},variant{a = 13},variant{b = 7},variant{b = 3}}
find x: int(0..100)
find y: int(0..100)
letting r be variant{a = 1}
find r_in_p: bool

such that

x = sum([ i[a] | i <- p, active(i, a) ]),
y = sum([ i[b] | i <- p, active(i, b) ]),
r_in_p = r in p
